 succeeded in 0ms:
          ENGINE_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ,
          this.adapter.caps.inputSampleRateHz,
        );
        this.adapter.sendAudio(resampled);
      }),
      bus.on<UserTextReceivedPacket>("user.text_received", (pkt) => {
        if (pkt.text.trim().length > 0) this.adapter.sendText?.(pkt.text);
      }),
      bus.on<TextToSpeechPlayoutProgressPacket>("tts.playout_progress", (pkt) => {
        if (pkt.contextId === this.contextId) this.playedMs = pkt.playedOutMs;
      }),
      bus.on<InterruptTtsPacket>("interrupt.tts", () => {
        this.adapter.cancelResponse(this.playedMs);
        this.inflight?.abort();
      }),
    );

    if (this.opts.syrinxTurns) {
      this.disposers.push(
        bus.on("eos.turn_complete", () => {
          this.adapter.requestResponse?.();
        }),
      );
    }

    await this.adapter.open(this.sessionAbort.signal);
    void this.pump();
  }

  async close(): Promise<void> {
    this.sessionAbort?.abort();
    this.inflight?.abort();
    for (const dispose of this.disposers.splice(0)) dispose();
    await this.adapter.close();
    this.bus = null;
    this.sessionAbort = null;
  }

  private async pump(): Promise<void> {
    const bus = this.bus;
    if (!bus) return;

    try {
      for await (const ev of this.adapter.events) {
        if (!this.bus) return;
        await this.handleEvent(bus, ev);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (!this.bus || isAbortError(err)) return;
      this.onError(
        this.bus,
        err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)),
        false,
      );
    }
  }

  private async handleEvent(bus: PipelineBus, ev: RealtimeEvent): Promise<void> {
    switch (ev.type) {
      case "response_started":
        this.onResponseStarted(bus);
        break;
      case "audio":
        // textOnly: provider audio must not be used (REQ-4) — TTS plugin owns playout.
        if (!this.opts.textOnly) this.onAudio(bus, ev.pcm16, ev.sampleRateHz);
        break;
      case "transcript":
        if (ev.role === "user") {
          if (ev.final) this.onFinalTranscript(bus, ev.text);
        } else if (this.opts.textOnly) {
          // Drive cascade TTS path as text arrives; do not buffer for display-only re-emit.
          this.onTextOnlyAssistantTranscript(bus, ev.text, ev.final);
        } else if (ev.final && ev.text.trim()) {
          this.turnAssistantText = this.turnAssistantText
            ? `${this.turnAssistantText} ${ev.text.trim()}`
            : ev.text.trim();
        } else if (!ev.final && ev.text) {
          // Streamed fragments already carry their own leading spaces — concatenate verbatim.
          this.turnAssistantDeltas += ev.text;
          bus.push(Route.Main, {
            kind: "llm.delta",
            contextId: this.contextId,
            timestampMs: Date.now(),
            text: ev.text,
          });
        }
        break;
      case "tool_call":
        await this.handleToolCall(bus, ev);
        break;
      case "response_done":
        this.onResponseDone(bus);
        break;
      case "speech_started":
        this.onSpeechStarted(bus);
        break;
      case "speech_stopped":
        this.onSpeechStopped(bus);
        break;
      case "resumption_handle": {
        // G4: persist-worthy native-resume handle (Gemini). Background route —
        // a durable host stores the latest and passes it back on reconnect.
        const packet: RealtimeResumptionHandlePacket = {
          kind: "realtime.resumption_handle",
          contextId: this.contextId,
          timestampMs: Date.now(),
          handle: ev.handle,
        };
        bus.push(Route.Background, packet);
        break;
      }
      case "error":
        this.onError(bus, ev.cause, ev.recoverable);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  private async handleToolCall(
    bus: PipelineBus,
    ev: { toolId: string; toolName: string; args: Record<string, unknown> },
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (ev.toolName === this.delegateToolName) {
      if (this.reasoner) this.dispatchDelegate(bus, ev);
      return;
    }
    if (this.opts.onFrontToolCall) {
      await this.handleFrontTool(bus, ev);
      return;
    }
    // No front-tool handler: with a reasoner, an unexpected tool is a recoverable error
    // (without one, tool calls are ignored, as before).
    if (this.reasoner) {
      const cause = new Error(`Unexpected tool call "${ev.toolName}" (expected "${this.delegateToolName}")`);
      if (this.opts.debug) console.error("[realtime-bridge]", cause.message);
      this.onError(bus, cause, true);
    }
  }

  private async handleFrontTool(
  private async handleFrontTool(
    bus: PipelineBus,
    ev: { toolId: string; toolName: string; args: Record<string, unknown> },
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.opts.onFrontToolCall) return;
    let result: string;
    try {
      result = (await this.opts.onFrontToolCall({ toolId: ev.toolId, toolName: ev.toolName, args: ev.args })) ?? "";
    } catch (err) {
      this.onError(bus, err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)), true);
      return;
    }
    this.adapter.injectToolResult(ev.toolId, result);
  }

  /**
   * Start the reasoner delegate OFF the serial event pump. The pump must keep draining adapter
   * events during the multi-second reasoner "thinking gap" — above all `speech_started`, so a
   * barge-in mid-delegate still fires `interrupt.detected` and (via `interrupt.tts`) aborts the
   * now-unwanted delegate before its answer is voiced over the user. Awaiting the delegate inside
   * the pump loop (the old shape) froze barge-in for the entire gap. `runDelegate` sets
   * `this.inflight` synchronously before its first await, so a subsequently-pumped barge-in sees
   * a live controller to abort.
   */
  private dispatchDelegate(
    bus: PipelineBus,
    ev: { toolId: string; toolName: string; args: Record<string, unknown> },
  ): void {
    this.delegateTask = this.runDelegate(bus, ev).catch((err) => {
      if (!this.bus || isAbortError(err)) return;
      this.onError(bus, err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)), false);
    });
  }

  private async runDelegate(
    bus: PipelineBus,
    ev: { toolId: string; toolName: string; args: Record<string, unknown> },
  ): Promise<void> {
    // Pin the turn's contextId now: the pump keeps advancing while the delegate streams, so
    // this.contextId may move to a later turn before we finish. The tool_call/tool_result pair
    // must stay bound to the turn that issued the call (R1: never merge parts across turns).
    const contextId = this.contextId;
    if (!contextId) return;

    const toolCall: LlmToolCallPacket = {
      kind: "llm.tool_call",
      contextId,
      timestampMs: Date.now(),
      toolId: ev.toolId,
      toolName: ev.toolName,
      toolArgs: ev.args,
    };
    bus.push(Route.Main, toolCall);

    const queryArg = this.opts.delegateQueryArg ?? "query";
    const rawQuery = ev.args[queryArg];
    const userText = typeof rawQuery === "string" ? rawQuery : "";
    if (userText.trim().length === 0) {
      this.onError(
        bus,
        new Error(`delegate tool "${ev.toolName}" called without a string "${queryArg}" argument`),
        true,
      );
      return;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.inflight = controller;
    let answer = "";
    let grounded = false;

    // G2 observability: the query is on its way to the reasoner (Background route, R4).
    const queryStartedMs = Date.now();
    const delegateQuery: DelegateQueryPacket = {
      kind: "delegate.query",
      contextId,
      timestampMs: queryStartedMs,
      query: userText,
      toolId: ev.toolId,
      toolName: ev.toolName,
    };
    bus.push(Route.Background, delegateQuery);

    try {
      const messages = this.opts.contextProvider?.() ?? [];
      for await (const part of this.reasoner!.stream({
        userText,
        toolArgs: ev.args,
        messages,
        signal: controller.signal,
      })) {
        switch (part.type) {
          case "text-delta":
            answer += part.text;
            break;
          case "tool-result":
            grounded = true;
            break;
          case "finish":
            if (!answer && part.text) answer = part.text;
            break;
          case "suspended": {
            const cause = new Error(
              part.prompt ?? "delegate suspended — cannot voice inline without resume",
            );
            if (this.opts.debug) console.error("[realtime-bridge]", cause.message, part.payload);
            this.onError(bus, cause, false);
            return;
          }
          case "error":
            if (!part.recoverable) throw part.cause;
            this.onError(bus, part.cause, true);
            return;
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (isAbortError(err)) return;
      this.onError(bus, err instanceof Error ? err : new Error(String(err)), false);
      return;
    } finally {
      // Only clear if still ours — a newer delegate may have replaced this.inflight.
      if (this.inflight === controller) this.inflight = undefined;
    }

    if (answer.length === 0) {
      this.onError(bus, new Error("delegate produced no output"), false);
      return;
    }

    const answeredMs = Date.now();
    const toolResult: LlmToolResultPacket = {
      kind: "llm.tool_result",
      contextId,
      timestampMs: answeredMs,
      toolId: ev.toolId,
      toolName: ev.toolName,
      result: answer,
    };
    bus.push(Route.Main, toolResult);
    // G2 observability: the reasoner produced the final answer (Background route, R4).
    const delegateResult: DelegateResultPacket = {
      kind: "delegate.result",
      contextId,
      timestampMs: answeredMs,
      query: userText,
      answer,
      durationMs: answeredMs - queryStartedMs,
      grounded,
      toolId: ev.toolId,
      toolName: ev.toolName,
    };
    bus.push(Route.Background, delegateResult);
    this.adapter.injectToolResult(ev.toolId, this.formatToolResult(answer));
  }

  /** G1: wrap the buffered delegate answer for the front model (synchronous — R2). */
  private formatToolResult(answer: string): string {
    if (this.opts.toolResultFormat === "string") return answer;
    const envelope: DelegateResultEnvelope = {
      response_text: answer,
      require_repeat_verbatim: true,
      ...(this.opts.renderDirective ? { render: this.opts.renderDirective } : {}),
    };
    return JSON.stringify(envelope);
  }

  private onSpeechStarted(bus: PipelineBus): void {
    if (!this.adapter.caps.emitsServerSpeechStarted || !this.contextId) return;
    const packet: InterruptionDetectedPacket = {
      kind: "interrupt.detected",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      timestampMs: Date.now(),
      source: "vad",
    };
    bus.push(Route.Critical, packet);
  }

  private onSpeechStopped(bus: PipelineBus): void {
    if (!this.contextId) return;
    bus.push(Route.Main, {
      kind: "vad.speech_ended",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      timestampMs: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  private onResponseStarted(bus: PipelineBus): void {
    const previousContextId = this.contextId;
    this.contextId = crypto.randomUUID();
    this.turnUserText = "";
    this.turnAssistantText = "";
    this.turnAssistantDeltas = "";
    this.playedMs = 0;
    this.audioRemainder = new Uint8Array(0);
    const packet: TurnChangePacket = {
      kind: "turn.change",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      previousContextId,
      reason: "realtime_response_started",
      timestampMs: Date.now(),
    };
    bus.push(Route.Main, packet);
  }

  private onAudio(bus: PipelineBus, pcm16: Uint8Array, sampleRateHz: number): void {
    if (!this.contextId) return;
    const resampled = resamplePcm16Bytes(pcm16, sampleRateHz, ENGINE_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ);
    this.audioRemainder = concatBytes(this.audioRemainder, resampled);
    this.emitCoalescedAudio(bus, false);
  }

  private onFinalTranscript(bus: PipelineBus, text: string): void {
    if (!this.contextId || text.trim().length === 0) return;
    this.turnUserText = text;
    const packet: SttResultPacket = {
      kind: "stt.result",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      timestampMs: Date.now(),
      text,
      confidence: 0,
    };
    };
    bus.push(Route.Main, packet);
  }

  /**
   * textOnly: stream assistant transcript into the cascade LLM→segmenter→TTS path.
   * Non-final deltas drive `llm.delta` immediately (no buffer). Final emits `llm.done` only —
   * the C0 adapter's final carries the full accumulated text, so re-emitting as delta would
   * duplicate every streamed fragment.
   */
  private onTextOnlyAssistantTranscript(bus: PipelineBus, text: string, final: boolean): void {
    if (!this.contextId) return;
    const timestampMs = Date.now();
    if (!final && text) {
      const delta: LlmDeltaPacket = {
        kind: "llm.delta",
        contextId: this.contextId,
        timestampMs,
        text,
      };
      bus.push(Route.Main, delta);
      return;
    }
    if (final && text.trim()) {
      const done: LlmResponseDonePacket = {
        kind: "llm.done",
        contextId: this.contextId,
        timestampMs,
        text,
      };
      bus.push(Route.Main, done);
    }
  }

  private onResponseDone(bus: PipelineBus): void {
    if (!this.contextId) return;
    if (!this.opts.textOnly) {
      this.emitCoalescedAudio(bus, true);
      this.audioRemainder = new Uint8Array(0);
    }

    const timestampMs = Date.now();
    const transcripts: SttResultPacket[] = this.turnUserText
      ? [{
          kind: "stt.result",
          contextId: this.contextId,
          timestampMs,
          text: this.turnUserText,
          confidence: 0,
        }]
      : [];
    const turnComplete: EndOfSpeechPacket = {
      kind: "eos.turn_complete",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      timestampMs,
      text: this.turnUserText,
      transcripts,
    };

    if (this.opts.textOnly) {
      // Already streamed llm.delta/llm.done above. TTS plugin owns tts.end (REQ-5) — forcing
      // it here would cut playout / break barge-in heard-prefix timing.
      // syrinxTurns: endpointing already emitted the user-turn eos.turn_complete that drove
      // requestResponse; re-emitting here would re-subscribe-fire requestResponse → loop.
      if (!this.opts.syrinxTurns) bus.push(Route.Main, turnComplete);
      return;
    }

    const ttsEnd: TextToSpeechEndPacket = {
      kind: "tts.end",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      timestampMs,
    };
    // Surface the assistant's spoken transcript as agent text so UIs (e.g. the studio) render it.
    // The realtime adapter already produced the audio directly; these packets are display-only.
    // Prefer the authoritative final transcript (OpenAI); fall back to the streamed fragments for
    // providers that only emit non-final deltas (Gemini Live).
    const assistantText = this.turnAssistantText.trim() || this.turnAssistantDeltas.trim();
    if (assistantText) {
      const done: LlmResponseDonePacket = {
        kind: "llm.done",
        contextId: this.contextId,
        timestampMs,
        text: assistantText,
      };
      if (this.turnAssistantDeltas.length === 0) {
        bus.push(Route.Main, {
          kind: "llm.delta",
          contextId: this.contextId,
          timestampMs,
          text: assistantText,
        });
      }
      bus.push(Route.Main, done);
    }
    bus.push(Route.Main, turnComplete, ttsEnd);
  }

  private emitCoalescedAudio(bus: PipelineBus, flush: boolean): void {
    if (!this.contextId) return;

    let buf = this.audioRemainder;
    if (buf.byteLength === 0) return;

    let processLen = buf.byteLength;
    if (processLen % 2 !== 0) {
      processLen -= 1;
    }
    if (processLen < 2) return;

    let offset = 0;
    while (offset + FRAME_BYTES_20MS <= processLen) {
      const frame = buf.subarray(offset, offset + FRAME_BYTES_20MS);
      this.pushAudioFrame(bus, frame);
      offset += FRAME_BYTES_20MS;
    }

    const trailingEven = processLen - offset;
    if (flush && trailingEven >= 2) {
      this.pushAudioFrame(bus, buf.subarray(offset, offset + trailingEven));
      offset += trailingEven;
    }

    const leftoverStart = offset;
    const leftoverEnd = buf.byteLength;
    this.audioRemainder =
      leftoverStart < leftoverEnd
        ? new Uint8Array(buf.subarray(leftoverStart, leftoverEnd))
        : new Uint8Array(0);
  }

  private pushAudioFrame(bus: PipelineBus, frame: Uint8Array): void {
    if (frame.byteLength % 2 !== 0 || frame.byteLength === 0) return;
    const packet: TextToSpeechAudioPacket = {
      kind: "tts.audio",
      contextId: this.contextId,
      timestampMs: Date.now(),
      audio: frame,
      sampleRateHz: ENGINE_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ,
    };
    bus.push(Route.Main, packet);

   491	  private onFinalTranscript(bus: PipelineBus, text: string): void {
   492	    if (!this.contextId || text.trim().length === 0) return;
   493	    this.turnUserText = text;
   494	    const packet: SttResultPacket = {
   495	      kind: "stt.result",
   496	      contextId: this.contextId,
   497	      timestampMs: Date.now(),
   498	      text,
   499	      confidence: 0,
   500	    };
   501	    bus.push(Route.Main, packet);
   502	  }
   503	
   504	  /**
   505	   * textOnly: stream assistant transcript into the cascade LLM→segmenter→TTS path.
   506	   * Non-final deltas drive `llm.delta` immediately (no buffer). Final emits `llm.done` only —
   507	   * the C0 adapter's final carries the full accumulated text, so re-emitting as delta would
   508	   * duplicate every streamed fragment.
   509	   */
   510	  private onTextOnlyAssistantTranscript(bus: PipelineBus, text: string, final: boolean): void {
   511	    if (!this.contextId) return;
   512	    const timestampMs = Date.now();
   513	    if (!final && text) {
   514	      const delta: LlmDeltaPacket = {
   515	        kind: "llm.delta",
   516	        contextId: this.contextId,
   517	        timestampMs,
   518	        text,
   519	      };
   520	      bus.push(Route.Main, delta);
   521	      return;
   522	    }
   523	    if (final && text.trim()) {
   524	      const done: LlmResponseDonePacket = {
   525	        kind: "llm.done",
   526	        contextId: this.contextId,
   527	        timestampMs,
   528	        text,
   529	      };
   530	      bus.push(Route.Main, done);
   531	    }
   532	  }
   533	
   534	  private onResponseDone(bus: PipelineBus): void {
   535	    if (!this.contextId) return;

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.

   827	  // plugin, so vad.speech_started never fires and barge-in would stay dormant.
   828	  // Provider transcripts arriving while TTS playout is active are the speech
   829	  // evidence instead (echo of our own playout is mitigated by client AEC plus
   830	  // the arbiter's backchannel / low-confidence suppression).
   831	  private observeSttForBargeIn(
   832	    contextId: string,
   833	    timestampMs: number,
   834	    text: string,

   867	    this.userSpeaking = true;
   868

   870	      this.cancelLatencyFillerTurn(this.currentTurnId, pkt.timestampMs);
   871	    }

   931	    });

   934	    this.timingFor(pkt.contextId).speechEndedMs = pkt.timestampMs;
   935	    this.watchdogs.startVaqiMissedResponseTimer(pkt.contextId, pkt.timestampMs);
   936	  }
   937	
   938	  /**
   939	   * A realtime front rotates its contextId when the provider starts responding
   940	   * (`RealtimeBridge.onResponseStarted`), so the user-side anchors (`speechEndedMs`, `eosMs`)
   941	   * are recorded under the PREVIOUS context while `tts.audio` — and therefore the
   942	   * `turn_latency` emit — lands under the new one. Without carrying the record across the
   943	   * rotation, `emitTurnLatency` finds no anchor and silently drops every native turn.
   944	   *
   945	   * Only fills gaps: anything the new context already recorded wins.
   946	   */
   947	  private carryTurnTimingAcrossContextChange(previousContextId: string, contextId: string): void {
   948	    const previous = this.turnTimings.get(previousContextId);
   949	    if (!previous) return;
   950	    this.turnTimings.delete(previousContextId);
   951	    const current = this.turnTimings.get(contextId);
   952	    this.turnTimings.set(contextId, { ...previous, ...current });
   953	  }
   954	
   955	  private timingFor(contextId: string): TurnTiming {
   956	    let timing = this.turnTimings.get(contextId);
   957	    if (!timing) {
   958	      timing = {};
   959	      this.turnTimings.set(contextId, timing);
   960	    }
   961	    return timing;
   962	  }
   963	
   964	  private emitTurnLatency(contextId: string, firstAudioMs: number): void {
   965	    const timing = this.timingFor(contextId);
   966	    this.turnTimings.delete(contextId);
   967	    const anchorMs = timing.speechEndedMs ?? timing.eosMs;
   968	    if (anchorMs === undefined) return; // text-injected or fallback turn — not a voice TTFA
   969	    const anchor = timing.speechEndedMs !== undefined ? "speech_end" : "eos";
   970	    const eouDelayMs =
   971	      timing.speechEndedMs !== undefined && timing.eosMs !== undefined
   972	        ? timing.eosMs - timing.speechEndedMs
   973	        : undefined;
   974	    const llmAnchorMs = timing.eosMs ?? timing.speechEndedMs;
   975	    const llmTtftMs =
   976	      llmAnchorMs !== undefined && timing.firstLlmDeltaMs !== undefined
   977	        ? timing.firstLlmDeltaMs - llmAnchorMs
   978	        : undefined;
   979	    const textAggregationMs =
   980	      timing.firstLlmDeltaMs !== undefined && timing.firstTtsTextMs !== undefined
   981	        ? timing.firstTtsTextMs - timing.firstLlmDeltaMs
   982	        : undefined;
   983	    const ttsTtfbMs =
   984	      timing.firstTtsTextMs !== undefined
   985	        ? firstAudioMs - timing.firstTtsTextMs
   986	        : undefined;
   987	    const attributedMs = [eouDelayMs, llmTtftMs, textAggregationMs, ttsTtfbMs]
   988	      .filter((value): value is number => value !== undefined)
   989	      .reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0);
   990	    this.emit("turn_latency", {
   991	      tsMs: firstAudioMs,
   992	      turnId: contextId,
   993	      ttfaMs: firstAudioMs - anchorMs,
   994	      anchor,
   995	      ...(eouDelayMs !== undefined ? { eouDelayMs } : {}),
   996	      ...(llmTtftMs !== undefined ? { llmTtftMs } : {}),
   997	      ...(textAggregationMs !== undefined ? { textAggregationMs } : {}),
   998	      ...(ttsTtfbMs !== undefined ? { ttsTtfbMs } : {}),
   999	      unattributedMs: firstAudioMs - anchorMs - attributedMs,
  1000	      ...(timing.llmCallCount !== undefined ? { llmCallCount: timing.llmCallCount } : {}),
  1001	      ...(timing.llmPassTtftMs !== undefined ? { llmPassTtftMs: [...timing.llmPassTtftMs] } : {}),
  1002	      fillerUsed: timing.fillerUsed === true,
  1003	      backchannelUsed: timing.backchannelUsed === true,
  1004	    });
  1005	  }
  1006	
  1007	  private handleTurnComplete(pkt: EndOfSpeechPacket): void {
  1008	    if (this.lastFinalizedContextId === pkt.contextId) {
  1009	      this.bus.push(Route.Background, make.metric(pkt.contextId, "eos.duplicate_dropped", "1"));
  1010	      return;
  1011	    }
  1012	
  1013	    // A backchannel ("uh-huh", "okay") uttered WHILE the assistant is still speaking
  1014	    // is not a turn (B4). Dropping it here does two things: it does NOT cancel the
  1015	    // assistant's in-flight answer (the supersede path below would otherwise kill it),
  1016	    // and it does NOT spawn a second LLM response to the backchannel (the double-reply
  1017	    // bug). A backchannel with no assistant currently speaking IS a real turn — only
  1018	    // suppress when another context's TTS is actively playing. (English-only classifier
  1019	    // today — locale-aware backchannels are a separate improvement.)
  1020	    const otherTtsActive = this.ttsPlayout.activeContexts().some((c) => c !== pkt.contextId);
  1021	    if (otherTtsActive && isBackchannel(pkt.text)) {
  1022	      this.bus.push(Route.Background, make.metric(pkt.contextId, "turn.backchannel_dropped", pkt.text));
  1023	      return;
  1024	    }
  1025	
  1026	    this.lastFinalizedContextId = pkt.contextId;
  1027	    this.interaction.reset(pkt.contextId);
  1028	
  1029	    // Re-arm per-turn guard state for the next turn. Transports with a stable
  1030	    // per-call contextId (telephony callSid) reuse one id across turns, so these
  1031	    // Sets must be cleared at the turn boundary or turn 2+ inherits stale flags:
  1032	    // - firstTtsAudioFired: else vaqi.latency_ms is never emitted again
  1033	    // - interruptedGenerationContextIds: else turn N+1's LLM/TTS packets are dropped after a prior barge-in
  1034	    // - fallbackInjectedContexts: else only one error fallback can ever be spoken per call
  1035	    this.firstTtsAudioFired.delete(pkt.contextId);
  1110	      if (timing) timing.firstLlmDeltaMs ??= pkt.timestampMs;
  1111	      if (this.latencyFiller.isActive(pkt.contextId)) {
  1112	        deltaText = this.latencyFiller.spliceLlmDelta(pkt.contextId, deltaText);
  1113	        this.bus.push(Route.Background, make.metric(pkt.contextId, "filler.spliced", "1"));
  1114	      }
  1115	    }
  1116	
  1117	    this.emit("agent_text_delta", {
  1118	      tsMs: pkt.timestampMs,
  1119	      turnId: pkt.contextId,
  1120	      delta: deltaText,
  1121	    });
  1122	    this.debugPush({
  1123	      component: "llm",
  1124	      type: "delta",
  1125	      data: { context_id: pkt.contextId, text: deltaText },
  1126	      timestampMs: pkt.timestampMs,
  1127	    });
  1128	
  1129	    this.bufferTtsText(pkt.contextId, deltaText, pkt.timestampMs);
  1130	  }
  1131	
  1132	  private handleLlmDone(pkt: LlmResponseDonePacket): void {
  1133	    this.generatingContextIds.delete(pkt.contextId);
  1134	    if (this.interruptedGenerationContextIds.has(pkt.contextId)) {
  1135	      this.ttsTextBuffers.delete(pkt.contextId);
  1136	      this.bus.push(Route.Background, make.metric(pkt.contextId, "llm.done_ignored_after_interrupt", "1"));
  1137	      return;
  1138	    }
  1139	
  1140	    const spokenText = this.flushTtsText(pkt.contextId, pkt.timestampMs);
  1141	    this.emit("agent_finished", {
  1142	      tsMs: pkt.timestampMs,
  1143	      turnId: pkt.contextId,
  1144	    });
  1145	    this.debugPush({
  1146	      component: "llm",
  1147	      type: "done",
  1148	      data: { context_id: pkt.contextId },
  1149	      timestampMs: pkt.timestampMs,
  1150	    });
  1151	
  1152	    // Start idle timeout after agent finishes
  1153	    this.bus.push(Route.Main, make.startIdleTimeout(pkt.contextId, Date.now()));
  1154	
  1155	    this.bus.push(Route.Main, make.ttsDone(pkt.contextId, Date.now(), spokenText));
  1156	  }
  1157	
  1158	  private bufferTtsText(contextId: string, text: string, tsMs?: number): void {
  1159	    const buffer = this.ttsTextBuffers.get(contextId) ?? { pending: "", emitted: "" };
  1160	    buffer.pending += text;
  1161	    const complete = takeCompleteVoiceText(buffer.pending);
  1162	    if (complete.text) {
  1163	      const timing = this.turnTimings.get(contextId);
  1164	      if (timing) timing.firstTtsTextMs ??= tsMs ?? Date.now();
  1165	      this.bus.push(Route.Main, make.ttsText(contextId, Date.now(), complete.text));
  1166	      buffer.emitted = appendVoiceText(buffer.emitted, complete.text);
  1167	    }
  1168	    buffer.pending = complete.remaining;
  1169	    this.ttsTextBuffers.set(contextId, buffer);
  1170	  }
  1171	
  1172	  private flushTtsText(contextId: string, tsMs?: number): string {
  1173	    const buffer = this.ttsTextBuffers.get(contextId);
  1174	    if (!buffer) return "";
  1175	    const tail = buffer.pending.trim();
  1176	    if (tail) {
  1177	      const timing = this.turnTimings.get(contextId);
  1178	      if (timing) timing.firstTtsTextMs ??= tsMs ?? Date.now();
  1179	      this.bus.push(Route.Main, make.ttsText(contextId, Date.now(), tail));
  1180	      buffer.emitted = appendVoiceText(buffer.emitted, tail);
  1181	      buffer.pending = "";
  1182	      this.bus.push(
  1183	        Route.Background,
  1184	        make.metric(contextId, isCompleteVoiceText(tail) ? "tts.final_text_flushed" : "tts.final_tail_flushed", tail),
  1185	      );
  1186	    }
  1187	    this.ttsTextBuffers.delete(contextId);
  1188	    this.latencyFiller.clear(contextId);
  1189	    this.firstLlmDeltaReceived.delete(contextId);
  1190	    return buffer.emitted.trim();
  1191	  }
  1192	
  1193	  private cancelLatencyFillerTurn(contextId: string, timestampMs: number): void {
  1194	    const cancelled = this.latencyFiller.cancel(contextId);
  1195	    if (!cancelled) return;
  1196	    this.bus.push(Route.Background, make.metric(contextId, "filler.cancelled", cancelled.text, timestampMs));
  1197	    this.turnArbiter.emitInterruptDetected(contextId);
  1198	  }
  1199	
  1200	  private handleLlmToolCall(pkt: LlmToolCallPacket): void {
  1201	    this.emit("agent_tool_call", {
  1202	      tsMs: pkt.timestampMs,
  1203	      turnId: pkt.contextId,
  1204	      id: pkt.toolId,
  1205	      name: pkt.toolName,
  1206	      args: pkt.toolArgs,
  1207	    });
  1208	    this.debugPush({
  1209	      component: "tool",
  1210	      type: "call_started",
  1211	      data: {
  1212	        context_id: pkt.contextId,
  1213	        tool_id: pkt.toolId,
  1214	        tool_name: pkt.toolName,
  1215	      },

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.

diff --git a/runs/slice1c/slice1c-scratchpad.md b/runs/slice1c/slice1c-scratchpad.md

--- /dev/null
+++ b/runs/slice1c/slice1c-scratchpad.md
@@ -0,0 +1,6 @@
+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.

codex
The post-fix live acceptance could not reach the providers: the required launcher fails on the sandbox’s denied Unix pipe, and the loader fallback reaches the app but fails DNS for `api.openai.com` (native) and `api.deepgram.com` (cascade). I’m doing the final diff hygiene check now; the repository already contains a prior cascade proof at `runs/spike-turn-decomposition.txt`, but I will not present it as a fresh rerun.

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.
+- Verification: scoped tests 295/69/46 and all three typechecks pass. Live native/cascade runs are blocked by sandbox IPC/DNS (`api.openai.com`, `api.deepgram.com`); no provider output was claimed.

   454	    };

   456	  }
   457	
   458	  private onSpeechStopped(bus: PipelineBus): void {
   459	    const timestampMs = Date.now();
   460	    if (!this.contextId) {
   461	      // Server VAD reports speech end before response.created mints the response context.
   462	      this.pendingSpeechEndedAtMs = timestampMs;
   463	      return;
   464	    }
   465	    bus.push(Route.Main, {
   466	      kind: "vad.speech_ended",
   467	      contextId: this.contextId,
   468	      timestampMs,
   469	    });
   470	  }
   471	
   472	  private onResponseStarted(bus: PipelineBus): void {
   473	    const previousContextId = this.contextId;
   474	    this.contextId = crypto.randomUUID();
   475	    this.turnUserText = "";
   476	    this.turnAssistantText = "";
   477	    this.turnAssistantDeltas = "";
   478	    this.playedMs = 0;
   479	    this.audioRemainder = new Uint8Array(0);
   480	    if (this.pendingSpeechEndedAtMs !== null) {
   481	      bus.push(Route.Main, {
   482	        kind: "vad.speech_ended",
   483	        contextId: this.contextId,
   484	        timestampMs: this.pendingSpeechEndedAtMs,
   485	      });
   486	      this.pendingSpeechEndedAtMs = null;
   487	    }
   488	    const packet: TurnChangePacket = {
   489	      kind: "turn.change",
   490	      contextId: this.contextId,
   930	    bus.stop();
   931	    await started;
   932	  });

+# Slice 1c scratchpad
+
+- Repro: native `speech_stopped` can arrive before `response_started`; the bridge has no context at that point and currently drops it.
+- Adapter mapping is present and covered by `openai-compatible-realtime.test.ts`.
+- Fix: preserve a pre-response speech-end timestamp, bind it to the response context on `response_started`, then let the existing context carry-forward merge subsequent stages.
+- Regression shape now covers both boundaries: speech-end before the first response context, then assistant text on that context, then a second response context before first audio.
+- Verification: scoped tests 295/69/46 and all three typechecks pass. Live native/cascade runs are blocked by sandbox IPC/DNS (`api.openai.com`, `api.deepgram.com`); no provider output was claimed.

codex
Implemented the native fix in [realtime-bridge.ts](/Users/mithushancj/Documents/asyncdot-openscoped/voice-media-transport/syrinx/packages/realtime/src/realtime-bridge.ts:458): speech-end is buffered until `response_started` creates a context, then carried across subsequent context rotation. Added regression coverage for the real two-rotation sequence.

Diagnosis: (c) was true. The adapter test confirms raw `input_audio_buffer.speech_stopped` becomes `RealtimeEvent("speech_stopped")`; the pre-fix regression timed out because the bridge’s empty-context guard dropped it.

Verified:

- Core: 295 tests; realtime: 69; aisdk: 46 — all pass.
- All three typechecks pass.
- GitHub API unavailable.
- Live native/cascade runs could not reach providers: `tsx` hit sandbox `EPERM`; loader fallback hit DNS failures for `api.openai.com` and `api.deepgram.com`. Therefore no fresh live `turn_latency` output can honestly be claimed.
